*Mehmet Batuhan Özdaş,Murat Osmanoğlu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DWT-Fusion框架，利用离散小波变换分析代理语言模型生成的词元级对数概率序列，通过多分辨率信号表示提取局部概率动态作为检测信号。结合四种无需训练的投票策略（如校准加权软投票），在多个数据集上实现高精度检测，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练方法通常依赖全局统计特征，忽略了词元级可预测性中的局部和多尺度变化信息，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 采用离散小波变换对代理语言模型输出的词元级对数概率序列进行多分辨率分析，提取局部概率动态作为检测信号，并通过四种无需训练的投票机制融合多个小波配置结果。

Result: 在HC3、M4和MAGE数据集上，最佳单个小波配置的AUROC分别为0.9872、0.8185和0.7138；通过校准加权投票，进一步提升至0.9919、0.8477和0.7471，表明该方法具有优异的泛化能力和可解释性。

Conclusion: DWT-Fusion通过多分辨率信号分析与校准引导的投票融合，为无训练场景下的LLM生成文本检测提供了有效且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: Detecting LLM-generated text remains challenging under zero-shot and training-free conditions, especially when detectors must generalize across datasets, domains, and unseen generators. While existing training-free approaches exploit language-model statistics as detection signals, they typically characterize a text through global measures that summarize overall model behavior. Consequently, potentially informative local and multiscale variations in token-level predictability may remain underutilized. Motivated by this observation, we introduce DWT-Fusion, a training-free signal-based framework for detecting LLM-generated text using discrete wavelet analysis of token-level log-probability sequences produced by a proxy causal language model. The proposed framework analyzes these sequences through wavelet-based multiresolution signal representations and derives detection signals from localized probability dynamics. We further evaluate four training-free voting variants, including equal-weight hard voting, equal-weight soft voting, calibration-weighted hard voting, and calibration-weighted soft voting, to combine multiple wavelet configurations without training a supervised meta-classifier. We evaluate the framework on HC3, M4, and MAGE using GPT-Neo-2.7B, GPT-J-6B, Falcon-7B, and LLaMA-3-8B as proxy models. The best single wavelet configurations achieve AUROC values of 0.9872, 0.8185, and 0.7138 on HC3, M4, and MAGE, respectively. With calibration-weighted voting, the best ensemble variants further improve AUROC to 0.9919, 0.8477, and 0.7471. These findings show that DWT-based multiresolution scoring and calibration-guided voting fusion provide effective and interpretable signals for training-free LLM-generated text detection.

</details>


### [70] [Enough is as good as a feast: A Comprehensive Analysis of How Reinforcement Learning Mitigates Task Conflicts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22039)
*Zixuan Ren,Jinliang Lu,Junhong Wu,Yang Zhao,Dai Dai,Hua Wu,Haifeng Wang,Chengqing Zong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统比较了基于强化学习（RL）训练的大语言模型与传统监督微调（SFT）模型在模型合并中的表现，发现RL训练能显著降低任务冲突，减少合并后的性能退化。通过实证与理论分析，揭示了三个关键原因：(1) RL的在线策略训练数据控制梯度更新幅度较小，降低对其他任务知识的覆盖风险；(2) RL优化目标“足够即好”促使模型收敛过程中逐步减少冲突参数更新的幅度与数量；(3) RL联合优化正负样本，引导模型进入无偏的任务特定参数子空间，增强鲁棒性并避免参数冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于提升已训练模型的合并性能，但对不同训练范式（如SFT与RL）对模型合并效果的影响缺乏深入探讨。本文旨在揭示强化学习训练为何更适用于模型合并。

Method: 通过在五个代表性任务上进行系统评估，对比RL与SFT训练模型的合并表现，并结合大量实证实验与理论分析，探究其背后机制。

Result: RL训练的模型在合并后表现出更低的任务冲突和更小的性能退化，且其训练特性（如梯度控制、优化目标设计、正负样本联合优化）显著提升了合并适应性。

Conclusion: 强化学习训练范式因其独特的训练机制，使大语言模型在合并过程中更具优势，是实现高效、稳定模型融合的理想选择。

Abstract: Model merging plays a crucial role in consolidating multiple specialized models into a single, unified model, especially in the era of large language models (LLMs). Recent research has primarily focused on developing strategies to enhance merging performance with the trained models, while the impact of training paradigms, such as supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), on the effectiveness of model merging remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically explore the merging behavior of RL-trained LLMs compared to those trained with traditional SFT. Through comprehensive evaluations across five representative tasks, we find that RL significantly reduces task conflicts and results in less performance degradation after merging, making RL-trained models particularly well-suited for this process. To unearth the reasons behind the superior suitability of RL for model merging, we conduct extensive empirical experiments and theoretical analyses. Our findings highlight three key factors: (1) On-policy training data in RL control the gradient updates in a smaller magnitude, reducing the risk of overwriting existing knowledge for other tasks in the model. (2) The RL optimization objective, which favors ``\textit{enough is as good as a feast}", progressively reduces the magnitude and the number of conflict parameter updates as the model converges. (3) Joint optimization of positive and negative examples in RL steers the model towards an unbiased task-specific parameter subspace, ensuring robust performance while further preventing parameter conflicts.

</details>


### [71] [Developing and Validating the Spanish Version of the Large Language Models Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22041)
*Tran Gia Bao,Mo El-Haj,Sameha Al-Shakhsi,Antonio Garcia-Cabot,Raian Ali,Ala Yankouskaya*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究首次验证了西班牙语版大语言模型依赖量表（LLM-D12-SP）的信效度，涵盖工具性依赖和关系性依赖两个维度。基于386名西班牙语参与者的数据，确认性因子分析支持原双因素结构，内部一致性良好（总α=0.89），区分效度显著，且与网络成瘾、对LLM的信任感呈正相关，与认知需求无关。该结果为跨语言心理测量提供了支持，适用于组织情境中对LLM使用心理机制的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在工作和组织场景中的广泛应用，亟需可靠且文化适配的心理依赖评估工具，尤其针对快速采用LLM的西班牙语人群，而现有量表在该群体中仍属空白。

Method: 采用问卷调查法，对386名西班牙语参与者施测西班牙语版LLM-D12量表，通过确认性因子分析检验结构效度，计算克朗巴哈α系数评估内部一致性，开展区分效度和外部效度分析。

Result: 量表支持原始双因素结构，总分及两个子量表均具有良好的内部一致性（α≥0.85），两个维度具有区分效度；外效度显示其与网络成瘾和对LLM的信任正相关，与认知需求无关。

Conclusion: LLM-D12-SP在西班牙语人群中具有良好的心理测量特性，结合英语和阿拉伯语版本的验证结果，确立了该量表在跨语言环境下的适用性，为组织中研究人类对大语言模型的心理依赖提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: There is a growing need for reliable and culturally validated instruments to assess psychological dependency on large language models (LLMs), particularly as LLMs are increasingly used for task execution, decision-making, and communication in organizational and work-related settings. This need is especially relevant for Spanish-speaking populations, where LLM adoption is rapidly expanding, yet validated psychometric tools remain scarce. The present study reports the first validation of the Spanish version of the Large Language Model Dependency Scale (LLM-D12-SP), extending prior validations conducted in English- and Arabic-speaking samples. The LLM-D12 is a two-dimensional instrument assessing Instrumental Dependency (reliance on LLMs for performing tasks and supporting decisions) and Relationship Dependency (psychological reliance on LLMs for companionship and social interaction). A total of 386 Spanish-speaking participants (M = 28.0 years, SD = 6.1; 55% male) completed the LLM-D12-SP. Confirmatory factor analysis supported the original two-factor structure. The scale demonstrated good internal consistency (Cronbach's alpha = 0.89 total; 0.86 Instrumental; 0.85 Relationship). Discriminant validity analyses indicated that the two subscales represent related but distinct constructs. External validation showed that both dependency dimensions were positively associated with internet addiction and perceived trustworthiness of LLMs, while showing weak or no association with need for cognition. Together with prior English and Arabic validations, these findings establish cross-linguistic support for the scale's structure and provide a psychometrically sound tool for investigating psychological aspects of LLM use in organizational contexts.

</details>


### [72] [Scaling Native Multimodal Pre-Training From Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22043)
*Haoyuan Wu,Aoqi Wu,Hai Wang,Jiajia Wu,Jinxiang Ou,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在固定计算预算下，基于Transformer的视觉-语言模型的最佳模型规模和标记数量。发现最小目标损失遵循可预测的计算定律，而最优模型规模和标记数量则呈幂律增长。语言与多模态目标表现出不同的缩放行为：语言目标的分配法则对数据组成不敏感，而多模态目标则高度依赖数据组成。通过建模数据组成对计算定律和分配指数的影响，提出了一个效率前沿，明确指定了模型规模、标记数量和数据混合比例的精确配置。下游评估表明，原生多模态预训练能促进正向跨模态迁移，提升纯文本空间推理能力，并实现稳健的多模态上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型依赖纯文本预训练，限制了对多模态物理世界的感知能力。虽然原生多模态预训练能实现深度跨模态融合并缓解传统后期融合架构中的优化不对称性，但其缩放特性尚未系统研究。因此，亟需在固定计算预算下确定最优模型规模和数据量，以指导高效多模态模型训练。

Method: 在固定计算预算条件下，系统地实验不同模型规模与标记数量组合，分析语言与多模态目标的损失变化；通过拟合幂律关系，推导出计算定律与资源分配法则；利用数据组成变化建模影响，构建效率前沿；并通过下游任务验证跨模态迁移效果。

Result: 最小损失遵循可预测的计算定律；最优模型规模与标记数量随计算资源呈幂律增长；语言目标的分配法则对数据组成不敏感，而多模态目标则高度敏感；提出效率前沿可指导具体配置；下游任务显示原生多模态预训练显著提升文本空间推理与多模态上下文学习能力。

Conclusion: 本研究为可预测地扩展多模态基础模型提供了实证基础，揭示了多模态预训练中资源分配与数据组成的内在规律，为未来高效多模态模型设计提供了关键指导。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable reasoning capabilities, their reliance on text-only pre-training restricts the perception of the multimodal physical world. Native multimodal pre-training avoids this limitation by training models from scratch on multimodal inputs, thereby achieving deep cross-modal integration and mitigating optimization asymmetries inherent to traditional late-fusion architectures. Despite these advantages, the scaling properties of this paradigm remain systematically uncharacterized. To address this gap, we investigate the optimal model size and token count for training a transformer-based vision-language model under a fixed computational budget. We demonstrate that minimal objective loss adheres to a predictable compute law, whereas compute-optimal model sizes and token counts scale as power laws. Notably, language and multimodal objectives manifest distinct scaling behaviors. The language allocation law is largely invariant to the composition of the data, indicating stable language learning regardless of the multimodal data ratio. Conversely, the multimodal allocation law is highly sensitive to this composition. Specifically, text-heavy mixtures become compute-efficient only at larger model scales, shifting the optimal resource allocation toward greater model capacity. Additionally, by modeling the influence of data composition on compute laws and allocation exponents, we derive an efficiency frontier specifying precise configurations of model size, token count, and data mixture. Downstream evaluations further reveal that native multimodal pre-training induces positive cross-modal transfer, thereby enhancing pure-text spatial reasoning and enabling robust multimodal in-context learning. In summary, this empirical research establishes the essential groundwork for predictably scaling multimodal foundation models.

</details>


### [73] [Benchmarking Fine-tuning and Retrieval Strategies for a Multimodal Language Model on the NRC Reactor Operator Licensing Examination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22067)
*Isak Hwang,Yoon Pyo Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了310亿参数的开源多模态模型Gemma 4 31B-IT在核能领域的知识应用能力，通过对比八种模型-检索配置在美核管会（NRC）反应堆操作员执照考试中的表现。结果表明，采用监督微调（SFT）结合固定大小滑动窗口分块的检索增强生成（RAG）配置在14份通用基础考试中成功通过8份，优于其他所有配置；无微调的配置均未通过任何考试。总体准确率达79.7%，在阈值附近，压水堆（PWR）项目准确率高达80.2%。研究还发现：分块策略偏好随模型训练状态变化，且在匹配搜索环境时，检索增强微调（RAFT）表现不如标准SFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将大语言模型（LLMs）应用于核能行业需确保其输出基于领域专业知识。当前缺乏对大型模型在真实核能考试中表现的系统评估，尤其在结合微调与检索技术方面的有效性尚不明确。因此，亟需验证何种方法组合可使模型达到操作员级别的专业能力。

Method: 采用美国能源部《基础手册》作为知识源，使用BM25稀疏检索构建检索管道，并比较两种分块策略（固定大小滑动窗口与结构感知分块）。测试八种模型-检索配置，包括基础模型、监督微调（SFT）、检索增强生成（RAG）和检索增强微调（RAFT），并在2015–2021年期间的14份通用基础考试（7个压水堆、7个沸水堆）上进行评估，以80%人类通过率为基准。

Result: SFT结合固定大小滑动窗口分块的RAG配置在14份考试中通过8份，为最优方案；所有未经过微调的配置均未通过任何考试。总体平均准确率为79.7%，置信区间覆盖80%阈值，压水堆专项准确率达80.2%。此外，分块策略偏好随模型训练状态逆转，且RAFT在匹配搜索环境下表现弱于标准SFT。

Conclusion: 有效的核能领域大模型应用依赖于合适的微调与检索组合。本研究表明，结合监督微调与固定大小分块的检索增强生成是实现操作员级能力的关键路径，而检索增强微调在实际搜索场景中并不具备优势。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) into the nuclear power industry requires outputs grounded in domain-specific knowledge. This study evaluates a 31-billion-parameter open-weight multimodal model (Gemma 4 31B-IT) on its capacity to apply nuclear knowledge by benchmarking eight model-retrieval configurations against the U.S. Nuclear Regulatory Commission (NRC) Reactor Operator licensing examination. We evaluate 14 Generic Fundamentals Examinations (GFE) from the 2015-2021 March sittings (seven pressurized and seven boiling water reactor exams) using the standard 80% human passing criterion. The base model is compared against configurations utilizing supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on Gemini-distilled chain-of-thought (CoT) rationales, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) with BM25 sparse retrieval over the U.S. Department of Energy Fundamentals Handbook, and retrieval-augmented fine-tuning (RAFT). Within the retrieval pipeline, we compare fixed-size sliding-window chunking against structure-aware chunking. The SFT configuration with fixed-size chunking RAG met the criterion on 8 of the 14 examinations, outperforming all alternatives, whereas no configuration without fine-tuning passed any. Aggregate accuracy reached 79.7%, with a confidence interval spanning the threshold, and 80.2% on PWR items specifically. Furthermore, two regularities emerged: the preferred chunking strategy reverses depending on the model's training state, and RAFT underperforms compared to standard SFT in matching search environments. These results demonstrate which combination of fine-tuning and search approaches achieves operator-level capabilities.

</details>


### [74] [FSE: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition by Fast-Slow Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22075)
*Yunan Zhang,Yang Fan,Heng Li,Xiangping Wu,Qingcai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FSE 是一种用于持续学习命名实体识别（CLNER）的快速-慢速专家增强型跨度基模型。它通过共享的快速专家高效过滤不可能的实体跨度，而特定任务的慢速专家仅对剩余候选进行分类，从而在保持知识共享的同时减少每个任务的学习负担。此外，引入长度衰减负采样策略缓解跨度不平衡问题。在 OntoNotes 和 FewNERD 合成数据集上的实验表明，FSE 在 CLNER 场景中达到最先进的性能，并验证了各组件的有效性、更快的收敛速度以及双专家机制的功能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习命名实体识别方法存在灾难性遗忘和跨任务共享信息不足的问题，亟需一种既能稳定学习又能保持灵活性的解决方案。

Method: 提出 FSE 模型，包含共享快速专家（捕获令牌级关联以过滤无效跨度）和任务特定慢速专家（对剩余跨度进行分类），结合长度衰减负采样策略以解决跨度不平衡问题。

Result: 在 OntoNotes 和 FewNERD 数据集上，FSE 实现了当前最优的 CLNER 性能，验证了各模块有效性、更快收敛速度及双专家协同工作的合理性。

Conclusion: FSE 通过快速-慢速专家设计与创新采样策略，在持续学习命名实体识别中实现了优异的性能与稳定性，为未来 CLNER 研究提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Continual Learning for Named Entity Recognition (CLNER) enable models to incrementally learn new entity types without forgetting previously acquired ones. However, existing methods suffer from catastrophic forgetting and insufficient exploitation of shared information across tasks. This paper proposes FSE, a Fast-Slow Experts enhanced span-based NER model for CLNER. The shared fast expert learns token-level links to efficiently filter out unlikely spans, while the task-specific slow expert performs span classification only on the remaining candidates. It stabilizes learning by promoting knowledge sharing across tasks and maintains plasticity by reducing learning burden at each task. A length-decay negative sampling strategy to mitigate span imbalance is also introduced. Extensive experiments on OntoNotes and FewNERD synthestic datasets demonstrate that FSE achieves state-of-the-art performance in CLNER scenarios, with effectiveness of each component, empirical evidence of faster convergence and expected functionality of both experts.

</details>


### [75] [MEUSLI: a Multilingual Projector for LLM-based ASR and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22100)
*Lorenzo Concina,Seraphina Fong,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MEUSLI 是首个开源的多语言投影器家族，将 Whisper 编码器与开源多语言大语言模型（LLM）连接，实现 28 种欧洲语言的全开源端到端自动语音识别（ASR）。它在高资源和低资源语言上均表现优异，并可通过持续学习轻松扩展至未见语言。此外，MEUSLI 还可应用于多语言语音翻译和话题识别，仅需每语言数小时的任务微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级投影器通常仅支持少数语言，尤其是英语，限制了多语言语音理解的发展。为推动开放科学和包容性，需要一个支持多种语言、完全开源的系统来连接预训练语音编码器与多语言大语言模型。

Method: MEUSLI 采用 Whisper 语音编码器与开源多语言 LLM 结合，设计多语言投影器架构，通过持续学习技术实现跨语言泛化能力，并利用少量任务特定数据进行微调以支持语音翻译和话题识别等下游任务。

Result: MEUSLI 在 28 种欧洲语言中实现了高性能的端到端自动语音识别，涵盖高资源与低资源语言；在新语言上的迁移性能良好；在语音翻译和话题识别任务中仅需少量标注数据即可达到有效效果。

Conclusion: MEUSLI 为多语言语音理解提供了强大的开源基础，支持可扩展、包容性的 SpeechLLM 研究与应用，推动了开放科学在语音-语言联合建模中的发展。

Abstract: Lightweight projectors are an established way to connect pre-trained speech encoders with large language models (LLMs), mapping acoustic features into token-level embeddings for tasks like ASR and spoken question answering. Existing systems, however, typically only support a few languages and are often limited to English. We introduce MEUSLI, the first open-science multilingual projector family that links a Whisper encoder with open-source multilingual LLMs, enabling fully open-source end-to-end ASR in 28 European languages. MEUSLI extends prior monolingual pipelines, delivering strong results across high- and low-resource languages. Using proper continual leaning techniques, MEUSLI can be easily extended to other languages not seen in training. We further demonstrate that the MEUSLI projector can be leveraged beyond ASR, enabling multilingual speech translation and topic identification with only a few hours of task specific supervision per language. Overall, MEUSLI provides a solid foundation for multilingual speech understanding tasks, supporting scalable and inclu- sive open-source SpeechLLM

</details>


### [76] [Dynamic Commonsense Coordination for Empathetic Response Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22136)
*Zhengyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DCC动态常识协调框架，通过SCE-AttnRes、AGCF和ICAD三个模块实现上下文与情境常识的动态整合、低相关性常识过滤及生成过程中的常识记忆动态检索，显著提升情感识别准确率与回复多样性，在Empathetic-Dialogues数据集上表现优于基线模型，并通过LLM盲评验证其回复的相关性、连贯性和信息量更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在理解与生成阶段复用固定常识表示，难以有效协调跨阶段常识知识，限制了共情响应生成效果。

Method: 设计DCC框架，包含残差式常识交互（SCE-AttnRes）、关联引导常识过滤（AGCF）和迭代式常识感知解码（ICAD），实现动态常识协调。

Result: 在Empathetic-Dialogues基准测试中，DCC提升了情绪分类准确率与回复多样性，保持相近困惑度；LLM盲评显示生成回复更具相关性、连贯性和信息量。

Conclusion: DCC通过动态协调常识知识，有效增强了共情对话生成能力，为后续研究提供了可公开代码的高效解决方案。

Abstract: Empathetic Response Generation (ERG) requires models to recognize users' emotions and generate empathetic responses. Commonsense knowledge has been shown to support such reasoning, yet existing approaches typically reuse fixed commonsense representations across understanding and generation, limiting their ability to coordinate such knowledge across different stages. We propose DCC, a Dynamic Commonsense Coordination Framework with three complementary modules: residual-based commonsense interaction (SCE-AttnRes) to integrate contextual and situational commonsense representations, Association-Guided Commonsense Filtering (AGCF) to down-weight low-relevance commonsense relations, and Iterative Commonsense-Aware Decoding (ICAD) to dynamically retrieve commonsense memories during generation. Experiments on the Empathetic-Dialogues benchmark show that DCC improves emotion classification accuracy and response diversity over the CEM baseline while maintaining comparable perplexity. An LLM-based blind evaluation further demonstrates that DCC generates responses with better relevance, coherence, and informativeness. The code and implementation details will be publicly available at https://github.com/Hanabi-Q/DCC-ERG.

</details>


### [77] [From Isolated Tasks to Structured Capabilities: A Multilayer Taxonomy for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22182)
*Shixin Fang,Jiachen Wo,Wenjuan Qin,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含14个能力领域和91个子技能的多层分类体系，基于人类认知科学而非大语言模型架构，分为原始、构建和整合三层。通过分析2023-2025年来自ACL、AAAI、ICML和NeurIPS的31,505篇论文，映射了15,934篇以大语言模型为中心的研究，发现语言-语义能力、推理、规划与决策、感知是主要研究焦点，而部分领域关注度低于2%。某些子技能在多数领域中普遍出现，且存在高协同效应（如心智理论与社会推理）。该分类体系有助于统一评估框架、识别覆盖盲区并支持可验证假设的生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型评估分散于各类任务和基准，缺乏以能力为核心的系统组织，导致跨研究比较困难、能力探测不清晰、覆盖盲区难以识别。

Method: 构建基于人类认知科学的多层能力分类体系，采用分层结构（原始、构建、整合）定义14个能力领域与91个子技能；通过多模型标注、共识与仲裁对15,934篇论文进行大规模映射分析。

Result: 语言-语义能力、推理、规划与决策、感知为最常研究领域；部分领域研究覆盖率不足2%；单一子技能在多数领域中普遍存在；心智理论与社会推理具有最高协同提升（lift=30.84）。

Conclusion: 将分析单位从孤立任务转向结构化能力，该分类体系可促进研究组织、覆盖审计、评估解释及诊断、训练与迁移的可测试假设生成。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) evaluation spans diverse tasks and benchmarks, yet evidence remains organized around tasks rather than the capabilities they probe. This fragmentation limits cross-study comparison, obscures capabilities tasks recruit, and makes coverage gaps difficult to identify.
  We introduce a multi-layer taxonomy of 14 capability domains and 91 subskills across Primitive, Constructed, and Integrative layers. Human cognitive science guides capability definition and organization, not LLM architecture. Layer assignments draw on developmental precedence and hypothesized functional support, while human-origin constructs are adapted to observable model behavior.
  To demonstrate operational utility, we screened 31,505 papers from ACL, AAAI, ICML, and NeurIPS between 2023 and 2025 and mapped 15,934 LLM-focused papers through multi-model annotation, consensus, and arbitration. Direct research attention concentrated on Language-Semantic Competence (3,551; 22.3%), Reasoning (3,388; 21.3%), Planning and Decision-Making (2,149; 13.5%), and Perception (1,954; 12.3%), whereas six domains appeared in fewer than 2% of papers. Within domains, the most frequent subskill had a median prevalence of 97.9% and appeared in at least 90% of papers in 10 of 14 domains. Language-Semantic Competence and Reasoning formed the highest-volume pair (n = 1,864; 11.7%; lift = 2.47), whereas Theory of Mind and Social Reasoning and Interaction showed the highest lift among pairs with at least 20 co-occurrences (n = 62; lift = 30.84).
  By shifting the unit of analysis from isolated tasks to structured capabilities, the taxonomy supports research organization, coverage audits, evaluation interpretation, and testable hypotheses for diagnosis, training, and transfer.

</details>


### [78] [Why Large Language Models and Humans Converge and Diverge in Evaluating Creativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22218)
*Pengzhao Lyu,Yeun Joon Kim,Hanlin Xiao,Yingyue Luna Luan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型（LLMs）在创意评估中与人类判断的对齐情况，发现其对齐程度取决于评估标准。LLMs主要依赖新颖性等内在特质，但在社会、市场和声誉等情境维度上与人类判断存在明显差异。不同LLM模型采用的标准各异，导致其创意判断结果不一致。当评估强调内在属性时，LLMs与人类判断较一致；而涉及情境信息时则易偏离。因此，选择不同的LLM作为评估工具会识别出不同的创意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对大语言模型（LLMs）作为创意评估工具的有效性存在争议，尤其缺乏对其评估标准与人类判断之间一致性成因的理解。本文旨在揭示LLMs在创意评估中的标准基础及其对实际判断的影响，以解释为何其与人类判断时有对齐、时有分歧。

Method: 通过三个研究，涵盖六种广泛使用的LLMs，分析其在创意评估中所依据的标准。研究1识别出各模型使用的评价标准并比较其与人类标准的重合度；研究2基于1,103个创意想法，检验LLM与人类评估的相关性及模型间差异；研究3基于1,195个案例，考察情境信息对人类与LLM评估的影响差异。

Result: LLMs在新颖性维度上与人类判断最接近，但在情境维度（如社会、市场、声誉）上明显偏离。各模型表现出独特的、差异显著的评价标准。具有更广泛标准的模型能更好地区分人类认为更具创造性的想法。情境信息显著影响人类评估，但对LLM评估影响甚微。

Conclusion: LLM与人类在创意评估上的对齐程度取决于评估所需证据类型及模型自身标准。当评估关注内在特质（如新颖性）时，对齐较高；当需要情境信息时，易出现偏差。因此，选择哪个LLM作为评估工具具有重要影响，不同模型会识别出不同的创意。

Abstract: Despite the growing use of large language models (LLMs) as creativity evaluators, evidence of their alignment with human evaluations remains mixed, raising the question of when and why their judgments converge with or diverge from human judgments. Across three studies and six widely used LLMs, we addressed this gap by identifying the standards underlying LLM creativity evaluation and examining their downstream implications. Study 1 showed that LLMs generally relied on a narrower subset of human creativity evaluation standards. Convergence with human standards was strongest in the novelty dimension, whereas divergence was clearest in the contextual dimension, which captures social, market, and reputational information. Moreover, each LLM exhibited distinct, model-specific standards that varied substantially in breadth. These differences in evaluation standards were reflected in actual creativity judgments. Study 2 (N = 1,103 ideas) showed that LLM evaluations were moderately correlated with human evaluations, and individual LLMs with broader standards better distinguished ideas humans judged as more versus less creative. Study 3 (N = 1,195) showed that LLMs were less sensitive to contextual information: such information significantly altered human creativity ratings but left LLM ratings largely unchanged. Together, our findings help explain the mixed evidence on LLM-human alignment, showing that alignment depends on the evidence a judgment demands and the standards each model applies. LLMs may resemble humans when evaluations emphasize intrinsic qualities such as novelty, yet diverge when judgments require contextual information. Selecting an LLM evaluator is therefore a consequential decision: different models, applying different standards, recognize different ideas as creative.

</details>


### [79] [Biomedical Machine Translation for Low-Resource Arabic-Script Languages via Cross-Lingual Transfer and LoRA Adapter Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22300)
*Abdullah Alabdullah,Arash Eslamighayour,Sarp Harbalioglu,Lifeng Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对阿拉伯语系低资源语言在医疗领域的跨语言迁移问题，利用阿拉伯语和波斯语作为高资源中间语言，提升四种严重低资源语言（达里语、普什图语、苏拉尼库尔德语、乌尔都语）的医学机器翻译性能。通过在小型解码器模型上使用LoRA微调，训练领域特定的中间适配器，并评估三种迁移策略：少样本上下文学习、最小监督适应和首次在此场景中应用的零数据LoRA适配器合并。结果显示，仅用500句监督数据即可使达里语达到接近中间语言水平（CHrF++ 41.01），乌尔都语也取得显著提升（28.88）；而适配器合并方法在无需额外数据的情况下，使达里语性能接近监督适应结果（相差仅3.5 CHrF++）。然而，普什图语和苏拉尼库尔德语因与中间语言结构差异过大，仍不足以支持临床级部署，揭示了跨语言迁移在结构距离过远时的局限性。值得注意的是，即使无目标语言生物医学数据，该方法对密切相关的语言仍表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决阿拉伯语系低资源语言在医疗领域缺乏生物医学神经机器翻译资源的问题，尤其是在缺乏标注数据的情况下实现有效跨语言迁移。

Method: 采用LoRA微调在小型解码器仅有的大语言模型上训练领域特定的中间适配器，比较三种迁移策略：少样本上下文学习、最小监督适应和零数据LoRA适配器合并。

Result: 仅用500句监督数据的最小监督适应使达里语达到近似中间语言质量（CHrF++ 41.01），乌尔都语也有明显提升（28.88）；零数据适配器合并在达里语上表现优异，仅比监督适应低3.5 CHrF++；但普什图语和苏拉尼库尔德语因结构差异大，仍不满足临床部署要求。

Conclusion: LoRA适配器合并在密切相关的低资源语言上表现卓越，即使无目标语言生物医学数据亦可实现高效迁移；然而，当目标语言与中间语言结构差异过大时，跨语言迁移能力受限，表明需结合语言相似性与数据可用性进行系统评估。

Abstract: We present a systematic study of healthcare-domain cross-lingual transfer to address the scarcity of biomedical NMT resources for Arabic-script languages. We use Arabic and Persian as higher-resource pivots to improve translation for \textbf{four severely low-resource} targets: Dari (Afghan Persian, a standardised variety of Persian), Pashto, Sorani Kurdish (Central Kurdish, a major standardized variety of Kurdish), and Urdu (closely related to Hindi). Using LoRA fine-tuning on small decoder-only LLMs, we train \textit{domain-specific pivot adapters} and evaluate \textbf{three transfer strategies}: few-shot in-context learning, minimal supervised adaptation, and, to the best of our knowledge, for the first time in this setting, zero-data LoRA adapter merging. Supervised adaptation with just 500 sentences achieves near pivot-language quality for Dari (CHrF++ 41.01) and meaningful gains for Urdu (28.88), while adapter merging reaches within 3.5 CHrF++ of supervised adaptation for Dari at zero additional cost. Pashto and Sorani Kurdish remain insufficient for high-stakes clinical deployment exposing the limits of cross-lingual transfer when structural distance from the pivots is too great. LoRA adapter merging works surprisingly well for closely related languages, even without target-language biomedical data.

</details>


### [80] [A Factorial Study of Synthetic Data Generation for Low-Resource Machine Translation using Grammar Books](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22376)
*Varun Ghat Ravikumar,Sina Ahmadi,Lena Jäger,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种利用大语言模型从语法书中提取语法规则、例句和词典，并生成合成平行语料库以微调机器翻译模型的流程。与以往在推理时将语法内容作为提示的方法不同，本方法通过合成数据进行微调，在三种语言（Kalamang、Tuatschin、Mandan）上均表现出优于基线的效果，尤其在Kalamang上75%的配置中取得提升，最高ChrF++得分提升达+8.8。通过96种配置的系统性实验，识别出影响性能的关键因素组合及其失效场景。研究证明静态语言文献可有效转化为机器翻译训练资源，为严重低资源语言提供实用的翻译工具路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多濒危语言缺乏用于机器翻译的平行语料，尽管存在描述性语法书。如何有效利用这些现有语言学资源来支持机器翻译，是解决低资源语言翻译问题的关键挑战。

Method: 使用大语言模型从语法书中自动提取语法规则、例句和词典，构建合成平行语料库，并用于微调机器翻译模型；通过系统性因子实验（目标词性、检索粒度、样本量）评估不同配置的效果。

Result: 在三种语言上，合成数据微调显著优于种子数据基线，其中Kalamang在75%配置中表现更优，最大ChrF++提升+8.8；Tuatschin在59%配置中提升，最大+5.3；Mandan最大+3.3。实验揭示了关键因素组合对性能的影响及局限性。

Conclusion: 静态语言学文档可通过大语言模型转化为可用于机器翻译微调的合成数据，为严重低资源语言提供了可行的翻译工具开发路径。

Abstract: Most endangered languages lack the parallel data required for machine translation, despite the existence of descriptive grammar books. We introduce a pipeline that uses large language models to extract grammatical rules, example sentences, and lexicons from grammar books and generate synthetic parallel corpora for fine-tuning-rather than feeding grammar content into prompts at inference time, as in prior work. Validated on three typologically diverse low-resource languages-Kalamang (Papuan), Tuatschin (Romance), and Mandan (Siouan)-we show that fine-tuning on synthetic data improves over seed-data baselines in 75% of configurations for Kalamang and 59% for Tuatschin, with best-case ChrF++ gains of +8.8, +5.3, and +3.3 respectively. Through a systematic factorial study across 96 configurations varying target part-of-speech, retrieval granularity, and sample volume, we identify which factor combinations drive gains and where they break down. Our results demonstrate that static linguistic documentation can be repurposed for machine translation fine-tuning, offering a practical path towards translation tools for severely under-resourced languages.

</details>


### [81] [grapheme-kit: Grapheme-Level Metrics and Text Processing for Multilingual NLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22456)
*Izzath Nisfer,Ashini Kavindya,Ovindu Atukorala,Purushoth Velayuthan,Menan Velayuthan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出grapheme-kit库，将词法距离、相似度和评估指标扩展到基于字素簇，改进了泰米尔语和僧伽罗语的字素处理，通过OCR案例证明字素级度量能更准确评估复杂文字系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词法度量基于Unicode码点，无法准确反映多码点表示单个字素的文字系统中的错误，需要更精确的评估方法。

Method: 开发grapheme-kit开源Python库，实现字素簇识别、字素组合/分解功能，并在OCR任务中应用字素级度量进行评估。

Result: 字素级度量在复杂脚本（如泰米尔语和僧伽罗语）的OCR评估中表现更准确，能更真实地反映文本错误情况。

Conclusion: grapheme-kit有效提升了对复杂书写系统的词法评估精度，为多码点字素语言提供了更可靠的分析工具。

Abstract: Existing lexical distance, similarity, and evaluation metrics operate on Unicode code points, which can misrepresent errors in writing systems where a single grapheme is represented by multiple Unicode code points. We introduce grapheme-kit, an open-source Python library that extends these metrics to operate on grapheme clusters instead. The library also provides improved grapheme processing for Tamil and Sinhala, including accurate grapheme cluster identification and grapheme composition/decomposition utilities. Through an OCR case study, we demonstrate that grapheme-level metrics provide a more faithful evaluation of complex scripts.

</details>


### [82] [Skill Self-Play: Pushing the Frontier of LLM Capability with Co-Evolving Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22529)
*Siyuan Huang,Pengyu Cheng,Haotian Liu,Tao Chen,Yihao Liu,Jingwei Ni,Shijie Zhou,Ziyi Yang,Gangwei Jiang,Mengyu Zhou,Yu Cheng,Xiaoxi Jiang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Skill Self-Play（Skill-SP）框架，通过动态技能控制器、提议者和求解器的协同进化，解决大模型自演化中任务多样性与验证可靠性之间的矛盾。该框架在工具使用和推理基准上显著提升模型性能，尤其对初始不匹配模型有显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自演化方法面临任务多样性与验证可靠性之间的根本矛盾：环境依赖方法反馈精确但领域狭窄，开放式生成虽拓展任务空间但缺乏可靠验证，易导致误导性奖励污染训练循环。

Method: 引入技能作为中间路径，利用动态路由实现特定场景下的深度可验证执行与开放任务多样性的平衡；设计包含提议者、求解器和动态技能控制器的协同进化框架，通过强化学习循环实现三者共同演化。

Result: 在工具使用和推理基准上的实证评估表明，Skill-SP能有效提升成熟模型的性能上限，并显著改善初始不匹配模型的表现。

Conclusion: Skill-SP通过技能驱动的协同进化机制，成功弥合了结构化验证与开放探索之间的鸿沟，为大模型自演化提供了高效且可靠的进化引擎。

Abstract: LLM training is shifting from manual design and annotation to interaction-driven self-evolution. However, existing self-evolutionary methods face a fundamental dilemma between task diversity and verification reliability: environment-bound methods obtain precise feedback but confine learning to narrow domains, while open-ended self-generation broadens the task space but lacks reliable verification, allowing misleading rewards to pollute the training loop. We identify agent skills as a powerful middle ground to reconcile this tension: each skill ensures deep, verifiable execution in a specific scenario, while dynamic routing across skills maintains open-ended task variety. Leveraging this insight, we introduce Skill Self-Play (Skill-SP), a co-evolutionary framework comprising a proposer, a solver, and a dynamic skill controller. Orchestrated via a reinforcement learning loop, these components co-evolve in a continuous self-play loop: the proposer generates challenging tasks conditioned on dynamically sampled skills; the solver explores candidate solutions to push its capability boundaries; and the skill controller collects execution feedback to update and expand the skill library. This interactive co-evolution effectively bridges the gap between structured verification and open-ended exploration. Empirical evaluations on tool-use and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Skill-SP, serving as a robust evolution engine, consistently pushes the performance ceiling of competent backbones while catalyzing striking turnarounds for initially misaligned models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Qwen-Applications/skill-self-play.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [Cloud-Native Evaluation-as-a-Service: A Microservices Architecture for Scalable AI Monitoring with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21623)
*Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EaaS提出一种云原生参考架构，将AI评估方法封装为六个无状态的Kubernetes微服务，涵盖置信预测、校准评估、漂移检测、公平性监控等，并通过实证验证其有效性。在多个基准测试中表现良好：置信度覆盖接近理论目标，对漂移检测灵敏度高，公平性监控揭示显著种族差异，且系统延迟低，具备可扩展性。目前尚无其他开源平台同时支持置信预测即服务、微服务化和DAG编排。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估工具缺乏集成性、可扩展性和灵活部署能力，难以满足生产环境中对模型可靠性与可解释性的要求。因此需要一种模块化、云原生的评估架构，实现多种评估方法的高效组合与自动化运行。

Method: 将六种核心评估方法（如有限样本修正的自适应预测集、RFF-近似最大均值差异漂移检测、基于引导法的公平性监控等）设计为独立的Kubernetes微服务，并通过基于DAG的流水线编排器进行任务调度，结合结果存储API实现数据持久化。

Result: 实验表明：置信预测覆盖率达理论目标±1.4个百分点；模拟干扰下影响小于1.5%；漂移检测在中位带宽下达到100%检出率，第一类错误控制在5%-8.5%；公平性监控在UCI Adult Income数据上发现种族间公平性差距达0.33；系统延迟低，部分服务响应时间低于2毫秒，适合批量处理。

Conclusion: EaaS展示了将先进评估方法以微服务形式部署于云环境的可行性与优越性，填补了当前开源平台在置信预测即服务、微服务架构与动态编排方面的空白，为可信AI的工程落地提供了可复用的参考框架。

Abstract: We present EaaS, a cloud-native reference architecture that operationalizes AI evaluation methods as six stateless Kubernetes microservices: conformal prediction with finite-sample-corrected Adaptive Prediction Sets, calibration assessment, drift detection via RFF-approximated Maximum Mean Discrepancy, fairness monitoring with bootstrap confidence intervals, a DAG-based pipeline orchestrator, and a result storage API. We validate four key methodological concerns. First, empirical coverage is consistent with the marginal conformal guarantee across K=50 random calibration/test splits, with mean coverage within 1.4 percentage points of the nominal target. Second, all four MMLU answer tokens appear in the top-20 logprobs with 0% imputation needed, and simulated imputation at 10% produces less than 1.5% coverage impact. Third, RFF-MMD achieves 100% detection power for mild and severe drift at the median heuristic bandwidth, with Type I error between 5-8.5%. Fourth, fairness monitoring on the UCI Adult Income dataset reveals significant demographic parity disparities by race (DP gap=0.33) with stable alerts across sequential batches. Conformal prediction and calibration services achieve sub-2ms p99 latency at batch size 100; RFF-MMD requires ~500ms suited for periodic batch monitoring. A comparison with four open-source tools suggests that, to the best of our knowledge, no current platform combines conformal-prediction-as-a-service, microservice decomposition, and DAG-based orchestration.

</details>


### [84] [On the Depth Scalability of Logic Gate Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21633)
*Taegun An,Dohun kim,Haebeom Lee,Changhee Joo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IALGNs introduce input anchoring to overcome depth limitations in LGNs, enabling consistent accuracy improvements beyond 100 layers on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100 by preserving a computational spine and supporting deeper, more informative representations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LGNs fail to benefit from increased depth due to optimization collapse and topology-induced limitations, even with stable training techniques; thus, deeper layers need both stable training and effective input information access.

Method: Propose Input-Anchored Logic Gate Networks (IALGNs) where each gate combines hidden features with direct input anchors, ensuring every layer is conditioned on the original input; use random-k anchor relaxation to improve anchor selection without weakening the computational spine.

Result: IALGNs achieve consistent depth--accuracy gains beyond 100 layers across multiple benchmarks, outperforming alternative LGN topologies that saturate or degrade; layer-wise probes and effective-depth analysis confirm improved representation quality and longer computational paths.

Conclusion: Scalable depth in LGNs requires not only stable optimization but also a topology that enables input-conditioned refinement through preserved computational spines.

Abstract: Logic Gate Networks (LGNs) implement computation through compositions of Boolean operations, yet unlike classical Boolean circuits, existing LGNs do not reliably benefit from increased depth. We identify two distinct causes: optimization collapse in deep relaxed LGNs and a topology-induced limitation that persists even when skip-biased initialization and straight-through estimation stabilize training. Thus, trainability alone is insufficient; deeper layers must also receive information that supports useful computation.
  We introduce Input-Anchored Logic Gate Networks (IALGNs), in which each gate combines an evolving hidden feature with a direct input anchor. This topology preserves a computational spine while conditioning every layer on the original input. We show that a depth-D path can depend on up to D+1 input bits and establish a strict path-wise depth hierarchy. Random-k anchor relaxation further improves anchor selection without relaxing the spine.
  Across MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CIFAR-100, IALGNs achieve consistent fixed-width depth--accuracy improvements beyond 100 layers, whereas alternative LGN topologies saturate or degrade. Layer-wise probes, topology ablations, and effective-depth analysis show that input anchoring produces progressively more informative representations and preserves longer computational paths. These results demonstrate that scalable depth in LGNs requires both stable optimization and an information-access pattern that supports input-conditioned refinement.

</details>


### [85] [MotifRole-Diff: Risk-Optimal Role-Aware Corruption for Masked Molecular Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21634)
*Tasfia Nuzhat Ornee,Elias Hossain,Ivan Garibay,Niloofar Yousef*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotifRole-Diff proposes a role-aware corruption strategy for molecular graph generation using masked discrete diffusion, improving validity and reducing FCD on QM9 and MOSES benchmarks by adapting masking rates to token roles based on denoising difficulty and impact.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Uniform corruption schedules in existing methods treat all molecular graph tokens equally, despite varying denoising difficulty and structural importance. This leads to suboptimal reconstruction and generation performance.

Method: Introduces a risk-optimal, role-specific corruption schedule that allocates masking budgets according to empirically measured denoising difficulty and perturbation impact across token roles, while preserving model architecture and lossless decoding.

Result: On QM9, validity increases from 0.905 to 0.944 and FCD decreases from 1.701 to 1.609; on MOSES, validity improves from 0.920 to 0.938 and FCD drops from 2.125 to 1.850. Role-wise diagnostics confirm better reconstruction across token categories.

Conclusion: Structurally informed, role-specific corruption is more effective than uniform schedules for molecular graph diffusion, leading to improved generation quality under matched compute conditions.

Abstract: Masked discrete diffusion for molecular graph generation typically applies a uniform corruption schedule to all tokens in a lossless graph-to-sequence representation, implicitly treating structurally heterogeneous molecular components as equally difficult and equally important to reconstruct. However, different molecular graph token roles exhibit substantial variation in denoising difficulty and their influence on the decoded molecule, motivating role-specific corruption strategies. We introduce MotifRole-Diff, a role-aware corruption process that allocates masking rates according to empirically measured denoising difficulty and graph-level perturbation impact while preserving the model architecture, clean sequence space, and lossless molecular-graph decoder. We formulate schedule selection as the risk-optimal allocation of a fixed masking budget across token roles. Our theorem characterizes optimality for the modeled role-weighted residual risk, while downstream generation performance is evaluated empirically. Under matched architecture, training budget, and sampling compute, MotifRole-Diff improves validity on QM9 from 0.905 to 0.944 while reducing FCD from 1.701 to 1.609, and on MOSES improves validity from 0.920 to 0.938 while reducing FCD from 2.125 to 1.850. Role-wise diagnostics further show improved reconstruction across molecular graph token categories. Together, these matched-compute results indicate that structurally informed corruption is a more effective masking strategy than uniform schedules for serialized molecular graph diffusion.

</details>


### [86] [Toward User-Conditioned Evaluation of Personal LLM Agents under Temporal Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21635)
*Pin Qian,Su Wang,Yihang Chen,Qiaolin Yu,Xiaoyuan Wang,Zhitong Guo,Zhicheng Wang,Junxian You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出个人代理评估需要一种新协议：在不同用户依赖的持久状态中重复相同的时间干预，以衡量故障在各组件间的传播。作者定义了四个条件：明确的时间干预、干预期间的持久状态、跨维度影响以及用户状态的变化。对现有公开基准的审计发现，没有一个基准完全满足这四个条件。文章提出了一个最小化基准设计和候选报告指标，旨在为未来个人代理评估提供具体要求和度量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理评估基准将记忆、工具调用、安全合规等能力孤立评估，无法反映个人代理在真实用户环境中的动态演化与跨组件故障传播。因此，亟需一种能体现用户依赖状态持续性与多维影响的新评估协议。

Method: 通过形式化定义四个核心评估条件（时间干预、持久状态、跨维度效应、用户状态变化），对公开基准进行聚焦审计；基于审计结果，提出最小化基准设计与报告指标体系。

Result: 在所审查的基准中未发现满足全部四条件的案例，揭示了当前评估体系的关键空白。提出了一种可操作的基准设计框架及配套报告指标，为未来个人代理评估提供标准参考。

Conclusion: 个人代理评估必须超越孤立能力测试，转向基于用户条件状态演化的综合评估。本文提出的框架为构建更真实、可靠的评估体系提供了基础。

Abstract: Personal agents maintain memories, learned skills, tool configurations, and policy state that evolve with each user. Existing agent benchmarks often evaluate these capabilities in isolation: tool benchmarks test invocation under fixed APIs, memory benchmarks test recall or forgetting, and safety benchmarks test static policy compliance. We argue that personal-agent evaluation requires a different protocol: replaying the same temporal intervention across different persistent user-conditioned states and measuring how failures propagate across agent components. We formalize this requirement as four conditions: explicit temporal intervention, persistent state across the intervention, induced cross-dimensional effects, and variation in user-conditioned state. A focused audit of public benchmark protocols selected by explicit inclusion criteria identifies several close cases. Under our explicitly narrow operationalization, we did not find a protocol in that audited set satisfying all four conditions. This claim is scoped as a focused gap analysis with bounded literature coverage. This position paper proposes a minimal benchmark design and candidate reporting metrics for user-conditioned adaptation. The result is a concrete design requirement for future personal-agent evaluation, with metrics used as reporting tools for that requirement.

</details>


### [87] [Measuring the Dependency Gap: Diagnosing Inter-Column Fidelity in Tabular Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21636)
*Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种依赖感知的合成表格数据保真度诊断方法，通过分解XGB-C2ST测试为边缘、依赖和数值-类别交叉成分，揭示了现有评估指标对列间依赖关系的盲区。实验发现，即使最先进的流匹配生成器（TabbyFlow/EF-VFM）也存在真实依赖差距，破坏依赖会显著降低少数类判别性能，且该差距无法通过增加模型容量或简单干预解决，表明问题根源在于缺乏直接的依赖监督而非模型结构或容量限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据评估指标对列间依赖关系不敏感，导致无法准确衡量生成数据的真实质量，尤其在少数类判别任务中，依赖结构至关重要但常被忽略。

Method: 提出一种依赖感知的保真度诊断框架，将强分类器两样本检验（XGB-C2ST）分解为边际、依赖和数值-类别交叉三部分，以全因子化参考（最差情况）和真实数据（最佳情况）为锚点，量化生成数据在依赖结构上的表现。

Result: 实验显示，尽管生成器在标准指标上表现良好，但在依赖结构上仍存在显著差距；破坏依赖会严重损害少数类识别能力；增加模型容量或引入简单修正手段均无法有效缩小该差距，说明问题本质是缺乏依赖监督。

Conclusion: 当前生成模型的依赖差距并非由模型结构或容量限制所致，而是由于缺乏对列间依赖的直接监督机制。未来工作需引入显式依赖建模或监督策略，才能真正提升合成数据在复杂任务中的实用性。

Abstract: Synthetic tabular data is valued for preserving not only each column's marginal distribution but the dependencies between columns -- structure that carries much of the discriminative signal for minority classes in imbalanced domains such as fraud and clinical risk. Yet the metrics most commonly used to certify synthetic tabular data are, we show, largely blind to inter-column dependency: a baseline that models every column independently (and therefore destroys all dependency) is judged indistinguishable from real data by the logistic-regression C2ST, and the pairwise Trend score is only partially sensitive. We introduce a dependency-aware fidelity diagnostic that decomposes a strong classifier two-sample test (XGB-C2ST) into marginal, dependency, and numerical-categorical cross components, anchored between a worst-case fully-factorized reference (all dependency destroyed) and a best-case real-data oracle. Applying it to a state-of-the-art flow-matching generator (TabbyFlow/EF-VFM), we find a real dependency gap that standard metrics miss; destroying dependency outright collapses minority-class utility, and the generator's residual gap carries a smaller, consistent utility cost. We then ask whether this gap reflects a structural limitation of mean-field generative objectives. It does not: consistent with recent recovery results for variational flow matching, the objective is asymptotically exact. Yet the gap is stubborn -- a 16x increase in model capacity does not close it -- pointing to the absence of direct dependency supervision rather than a capacity or structural limit. Consistent with this, and because the residual gap is higher-order, no cheap intervention closes it: not an in-model dependency mechanism, not post-hoc copula correction, and not the 16x capacity increase -- a caution for a field that assumes such fixes help.

</details>


### [88] [Quasi-Monte Carlo Initialization for Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21637)
*Julian G. Soltes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了准蒙特卡洛（QMC）权重初始化在现代基准环境中的元强化学习效果，通过多种采样方法界定种群搜索并从基础任务集中聚合最优先验。QMC元先验在推广到类似未见的连续控制环境时，相较于现代正交（SB3）默认设置表现出更好的训练收敛性；而在任务差异较大时，正交方向在无偏搜索中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高元强化学习在新环境中的训练效率和泛化能力，探索更优的权重初始化策略以加速收敛并提升性能。

Method: 采用多种采样方法进行种群搜索，基于基准任务集聚合最优先验，比较QMC与正交初始化在不同任务环境下的表现。

Result: QMC元先验在相似未见连续控制环境中显著提升训练收敛速度；在任务差异大时，正交初始化整体表现更优。

Conclusion: QMC权重初始化在特定任务迁移场景下优于传统正交初始化，但其优势依赖于任务相似性，正交初始化在多样性和无偏搜索中更具鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper explores the efficacy of quasi-Monte Carlo (QMC) weight initialization for meta-reinforcement learning within modern benchmark environments. Various sampling methods are used to bound a population-based search and aggregate an optimal prior from a baseline set of tasks. The QMC meta-priors show improvements in training convergence compared to modern orthogonal (SB3) defaults when extrapolated to similar unseen continuous control environments. In dissimilar tasks, the orthogonal orientation was globally superior for an unbiased search.

</details>


### [89] [Toward Goal-Agnostic Joint-Embedding Predictive Control of Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21644)
*Jonathan Gallagher,Roberto Guglielmi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对偏微分方程（PDE）的无目标控制框架，基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）。采用小型2D ViT编码器和动作条件化的潜在动态模型，离线训练后冻结，由模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器复用。研究发现，当可用时，将控制目标施加于显式物理可观测变量（在可注入条件下）优于直接最小化学习潜在空间中的欧氏距离（L²）。对于冻结潜在轨迹上的线性动能（KE）探测器，可实现0.989的R²拟合精度，且无需修改底层世界模型。在二维纳维-斯托克斯基准测试中，使用KE探测器规划将50集原生奖励从-12.08±0.86提升至-10.90±0.91（95%置信区间），同时降低最后四分之一速度场均方根误差（RMSE）从0.0765降至0.0692。在三个故意隐藏、不同且非周期的目标上，KE规划使后期场RMSE降低53%（0.0220对比0.0469），赢得全部30组对战。同一冻结模型还可用于稳定控制，通过直接调节动能实现2.7%的平均相对误差。尽管潜在探测器对测量噪声和缺失像素敏感，但结果支持潜行动态模型可在保持动态性的同时实现无目标控制，且经校准的可观测变量（前提是保证唯一延续性）是状态控制更优的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常依赖特定目标或奖励信号进行控制，限制了模型的通用性和泛化能力。本研究旨在构建一个无需预设目标、可复用的控制框架，利用物理可观测变量作为控制目标，以提高控制精度与鲁棒性，尤其是在复杂动力系统如PDE建模中。

Method: 采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），结合小型2D ViT编码器与动作条件化的潜在动态模型，离线训练后冻结。使用模型预测路径积分（MPPI）控制器，将控制目标从原始潜在空间的L²距离转移到显式物理可观测量（如动能）上，利用其在系统演化中的可区分性与可解释性。

Result: 在2D Navier-Stokes基准上，使用动能探测器规划显著提升控制性能：原生奖励提升约1.18单位（从-12.08到-10.90），速度场RMSE下降约9.5%；在三组未见目标下，后期场误差降低53%，且全胜对战。此外，对稳态配置的稳定控制达到2.7%的平均相对误差。

Conclusion: 潜行动态模型可在不依赖具体目标的情况下维持其动态能力，而通过引入具有唯一延续性的物理可观测变量（如动能）作为控制目标，能显著提升控制精度与泛化能力，为复杂系统控制提供新范式。

Abstract: We present a goal-agnostic control framework for partial differential equations (PDEs) built around a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA). The small 2D ViT encoder and action-conditioned latent dynamics are trained offline without a reward or downstream goal, frozen, and reused by a model-predictive path integral (MPPI) controller. We find that when available, the control objective is better applied to an explicit physical observable (provided injectivity) than to minimizing raw Euclidean distance ($L^2$) in the learned latent space. For a learned linear kinetic-energy (KE) probe on frozen latent rollouts we can reproduce held-out trajectories with $R^2=0.989$, while requiring no change to the underlying world model. On the PDE Control Gym 2D Navier--Stokes benchmark, using KE-probe planning improves the matched 50-episode native reward from $-12.08\pm0.86$ for latent-$L^2$ planning to $-10.90\pm0.91$ (95\% CI), while lowering last-quarter velocity-field RMSE from $0.0765$ to $0.0692$. Across three intentionally withheld, dissimilar, aperiodic targets, KE planning lowers late field RMSE by $53\%$ relative to latent-$L^2$ planning ($0.0220$ versus $0.0469$), winning all 30 paired episodes. The same frozen model also supports controls targeting stabilization around a steady configuration via direct regulation of KE achieving $2.7\%$ mean relative error. While the latent probe is brittle to measurement noise and missing pixels, we believe the results support the claim that latent dynamics can remain both dynamic and goal-agnostic while calibrated observables (granted they guarantee unique continuation) may be a better objective for state control

</details>


### [90] [Multi-Horizon Consistency as Geometry: When Latent Dynamics Contract, and When They Do Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21645)
*Kavya Bhand,Aadi Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了视频预测模型中多时域潜在一致性参数lambda对过渡几何的影响，发现提高lambda可显著降低预测误差和潜在扩张度，但在不同任务上效果受限，表明软一致性可使被动视频接近近收缩带，但该特性具有领域局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多时域潜在一致性（lambda）在视频预测中的作用，特别是其对系统动态过渡几何的影响，以指导模型设计与训练策略优化。

Method: 将lambda作为诊断控制变量，通过测量经验扩张代理L20,q95与20步预测误差E20，结合配对t检验、威尔科克森符号秩检验及中介分析评估其影响；使用随机力律建模和联合切片分析验证跨域一致性。

Result: 在Moving-MNIST上，lambda从0增至0.8时，L20下降至1.01±0.06（显著），E20减半（0.365→0.177），四组种子进入L<1区域；但在其他任务如Pendulum-v1、CartPole-v1和KTH Actions上未见类似效应，即使E20改善；中介分析显示lambda与扩张度高度相关（r-hat=0.94），且存在统一的线性关系曲线（L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82 eta）。

Conclusion: 软一致性可通过调节lambda推动被动视频系统进入近收缩带，但该能力受任务域限制，不具备普遍性；跨域统一由校准后的有效扰动强度eta_eff实现，表明潜在一致性对动态结构有定向调控作用。

Abstract: Multi-horizon latent consistency is a common training knob in video predictors and world models, but practitioners rarely know what it does to transition geometry. We treat lambda, the weight on multi-step latent agreement, as a diagnostic control and measure an empirical expansion proxy L20,q95 together with horizon-20 prediction error E20. On Moving-MNIST (n=6 seeds at the critical pair), raising lambda from 0 to 0.8 cuts L20 from 4.96 +/- 2.01 to 1.01 +/- 0.06 (paired t p=0.005, Wilcoxon p=0.031) and halves E20 (0.365 to 0.177, paired t p=1.1e-13). Four of six seeds cross L<1 at lambda=0.8. The same loss does not produce population L<1 on action-conditioned Pendulum-v1 or CartPole-v1, nor on KTH Actions video, even when E20 improves. An associational mediation analysis on MMNIST gives r-hat=0.94 (95% CI [0.88, 1.00], n=27, B=2000); lambda was not randomized. Defensive checks (architectural baselines, exogenous stress, WorldTest, MPC, scaling) mostly support a narrow claim: soft consistency can push passive video toward a near-contractive band, and that band is domain-limited. A stochastic-forcing law L20 ~ 1.23 + 1.82 eta at lambda=0.8 (bootstrap slope CI [1.73, 1.92], R^2=0.96) unifies control domains on the same curve via calibrated eta_eff. Complete joint slices at lambda in {0.4, 1.2} (30/30 cells, 5 eta x 3 seeds) show comparable linear L20(eta) slopes (~1.69 and ~2.00); we do not fit a continuous (lambda, eta) surface. We do not report DreamerV3 or TD-MPC2 returns.

</details>


### [91] [Adjustment Speed as a Safety Constraint for Nonstationary Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21646)
*Timothy Tomashevskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将调整速度作为非平稳强化学习中的安全约束，通过预测环境变化带来的适应需求，并与智能体的恢复能力对比，提前收紧动作集并激活动作级防护机制，以减少不安全行为。实验表明该方法在环境变化时显著降低短期安全违规，且防护机制对极端风险抑制更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法多假设环境平稳，未考虑适应速度对安全的影响；当环境随时间变化时，延迟适应可能导致瞬时不安全行为，因此需要将适应可行性纳入安全考量。

Method: 利用学习到的上下文表征和短时上下文预测，估计适应需求，并与智能体的可实现适应能力进行比较；当预测适应需求超过恢复能力时，主动限制动作空间并启用动作级屏蔽机制。

Result: 在非平稳驾驶环境中，所提方法显著减少了与上下文变化同步的短期安全违规；消融实验显示，动作级屏蔽对峰值和尾部风险抑制更保守，而优化层级调整进一步降低了特定切换条件下的违规率。

Conclusion: 适应可行性是非平稳强化学习中一个切实可行的安全原则，主动干预可在环境变化期间有效提升系统安全性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety in reinforcement learning under nonstationarity requires determining whether a learning system can safely adapt to forecasted environmental change within the required recovery horizon. Existing safe reinforcement learning methods typically assume stationary environments and do not explicitly consider adaptation speed as a safety concern. However, when environments evolve over time, delayed adaptation may result in transient unsafe behavior.
  This paper proposes adjustment speed as a safety constraint for nonstationary reinforcement learning. The central idea is to define safety in terms of adaptation feasibility: future states or regions may become unsafe when the adaptation required to remain safe exceeds the learning system's calibrated recovery capacity. The proposed framework uses learned context representations and short-horizon context forecasts to estimate adaptation demand and compare it with the agent's achievable adaptation capacity.
  When predicted adaptation demand exceeds the calibrated recovery capacity, the framework proactively tightens the admissible action set and activates an action-level shield to reduce unsafe behavior before violations occur.
  Experiments in a nonstationary driving environment show that the proposed approach primarily reduces safety violations in short-horizon windows aligned with context changes. Ablation studies further show that shielding is more conservative for peak- and tail-risk suppression, while optimization-level adjustment provides additional reductions in short-horizon switch-conditioned violations.
  These results support adaptation feasibility as a practical safety principle for reinforcement learning under nonstationarity and demonstrate that proactive intervention can improve safety during periods of environmental change.

</details>


### [92] [A Drift Stable Quantum Federated Learning for Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21647)
*Shanika Iroshi Nanayakkara,Shiva Raj Pokhrel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DUQFL-Prox是一种基于深度展开的量子联邦学习框架，通过自适应展开SPSA更新和近端项约束，提升异构分布式环境下的模型稳定性、泛化能力和客户端公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决量子联邦学习中因客户端数据异构和量子优化噪声导致的本地更新不稳定、客户端漂移及性能不公平问题，以支持隐私敏感的智能服务如金融欺诈检测和基因组分类。

Method: 采用深度展开的局部优化方法，每个客户端执行自适应展开的SPSA更新，并引入近端项保持本地模型与全局模型接近；同时设计轻量级控制器学习特定步长的优化参数，以提升聚合后的性能。

Result: 在金融欺诈和基因组分类任务上的实验表明，相比标准量子联邦学习基线，DUQFL-Prox显著提升了模型稳定性、泛化能力以及客户端间的公平性。

Conclusion: 深度展开的量子联邦学习可有效支持异构分布式环境中的可靠且公平的智能服务。

Abstract: Quantum federated learning enables distributed clients to train quantum neural networks without sharing local data, making it promising for privacy-aware intelligent services. Intelligent services in this context refer to privacy-sensitive distributed decision systems, such as fraud detection and genomic classification, where reliable and fair client-level learning is as important as the accuracy of the aggregate model. However, heterogeneous client data and noisy quantum optimization often cause unstable local updates, client drift, and unfair performance between clients. This paper proposes DUQFL-Prox, a drift-stable quantum federated learning framework based on deep-unfolded local optimization. Instead of using a fixed local optimizer, each client performs adaptive unfolded SPSA updates, while a proximal term keeps the local model close to the global model. A lightweight controller learns step-specific optimization parameters to improve post-aggregation performance. Experiments on financial fraud and genomic classification tasks show that DUQFL-Prox improves stability, generalization, and client fairness compared with standard QFL baselines. The results suggest that deep-unfolded quantum federated learning can support more reliable and fair intelligent services in heterogeneous distributed environments.

</details>


### [93] [Shallower ReLU Network Representations via Exact Linear Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21651)
*Kilian Rueß,Gennadiy Averkov,Florestan Brunck,Moritz Grillo,Christoph Hertrich,Georg Loho,Jack Stade,Moritz Stargalla,Matthew Sun,Martin Winter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了对于任意n≤10，n个实数的最大值可通过两层隐藏层的ReLU网络精确表示。通过将问题简化为有理数线性代数，构造出可计算验证的解。该最大值表示具有结构化的第一隐藏层，仅包含成对最大值，允许递归嵌入更大网络。基于此，对于n>10，最大值max_n可被表示为少于log₅(n)+1.5694层的网络。这一深度界也适用于所有连续分段线性函数（在ℝᵈ上），其中用d+1替代n。特别地，当d≤9时，所有连续分段线性函数在ℝᵈ上均可由两层隐藏层的ReLU网络表示。结果优于先前工作[2026, STOC]，后者仅给出max₅的两层表示和log₃(n−2)+1的层数上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究如何以最少的网络深度精确表示n个实数的最大值，尤其是通过ReLU神经网络实现高效、精确的表示，推动分段线性函数的网络表示理论发展。

Method: 通过对称性约简将问题转化为有限有理数线性系统，并利用计算机求解与验证；设计具有成对最大值结构的第一隐藏层，支持递归扩展；结合广义铰接超平面表示法，推广到一般连续分段线性函数。

Result: 实现了n≤10时的最大值精确表示；对n>10，提出更优的深度上界：⌈log₅(n/2)⌉+1 < log₅(n)+1.5694；并证明d≤9时所有ℝᵈ上的连续分段线性函数可用两层隐藏层表示。

Conclusion: 本研究显著改进了最大值函数及连续分段线性函数的ReLU网络表示深度上界，提供了可计算、可递归的构造方法，为深度学习中复杂函数的高效近似奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We prove that the maximum of $n$ real numbers is exactly representable by a ReLU network with two hidden layers for every $n\le 10$. The constructions are obtained by reducing the problem to exact rational linear algebra: after a symmetry reduction, the necessary cancellations are encoded in finite linear systems over $\mathbb{Q}$, which we solve and verify computationally. The representation of $\max_{10}$ has a structured first hidden layer consisting only of pairwise maxima, a feature that allows it to be recursively substituted into larger networks. We use this to show that for every $n>10$, the maximum $\max_{n}$ can be exactly represented with $\lceil{\log_5 (n / 2)\rceil}+1 < \log_5(n) +1.5694$ hidden layers. Via the generalized hinging-hyperplane representation [Wang, Sun, IEEE Trans. Inf. Theory 2005], the same depth bound holds for all continuous piecewise-linear functions on $\mathbb{R}^d$, with $d+1$ in place of $n$. In particular, every continuous piecewise-linear function on $\mathbb{R}^d$ for $d\le 9$ admits a two-hidden-layer ReLU representation. Our results improve on [Bakaev, Brunck, Hertrich, Stade, Yehudayoff, STOC'26]. In that work, the authors established a two-hidden-layer representation for $\max_{5}$ and an upper bound of $\lceil{\log_3 (n-2)\rceil}+1$ hidden layers for $\max_{n}$.

</details>


### [94] [Molt: A Scalable PyTorch-Native Training Framework for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21653)
*Jian Hu,Huiying Li,Hao Zhang,Binfeng Xu,Yifan Zhang,Shaokun Zhang,Hemil Desai,Michael Demoret,Pavlo Molchanov,Jan Kautz,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Molt 是一个基于 PyTorch 的轻量级训练框架，旨在降低智能体强化学习研究中的开发成本。它通过简洁、可读性强的代码结构，使研究人员能够轻松追踪和修改算法流程，同时支持多模态与专家混合策略的异步训练，并确保仅使用自身生成的令牌进行训练。在完全异步协议下，其性能与最先进的 Megatron 堆栈相当。项目已开源，提供完整配方和容器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体强化学习研究中，每次算法迭代都需在训练器、分布式后端和回滚胶水层中反复修改，导致研究者负担沉重。需要一个更简洁、可维护的框架来降低开发成本。

Method: 构建一个原生 PyTorch 的训练框架，将智能体视为普通程序，采用单个异步循环实现多模态与混合专家策略的训练，保证训练数据来自自生成的令牌，在模型语义和策略版本上保持一致。

Result: 在匹配的全异步协议下，Molt 在性能上与最先进的 Megatron 堆栈统计上相当，且代码更简洁、易于理解和修改。

Conclusion: Molt 通过设计上的简洁性实现了高性能与高可维护性的平衡，显著降低了强化学习研究的工程负担，为研究人员提供了高效、可推理的开发环境。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning research is constant algorithm modification, new estimators, new pipeline stages, new rollout schemes, and in mainstream frameworks each change threads through layers of trainer, distributed backend, and rollout glue: the cost lands on the researcher at every iteration. Molt is a PyTorch-native training framework built to keep that cost small: a codebase compact and clean enough for a researcher to hold in their head, and for an AI coding assistant to read and reason about in its entirety, so the algorithm flow can be traced and changed end to end. The agent is an ordinary program, and one asynchronous loop trains multimodal and mixture-of-experts policies while never training on a token it did not generate, consistent in tokens, policy versions, and model semantics. Leanness does not cost performance: under a matched, fully asynchronous protocol, Molt is statistically comparable to a state-of-the-art Megatron-based stack. Molt is open source and provides recipes and containers at https://github.com/NVIDIA-NeMo/labs-molt.

</details>


### [95] [Physically Constrained Federated Additive Models for O-RAN SLA-Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21665)
*Aubida A. Al-Hameed,Mohammed M. H. Qazzaz,Maryam Hafeez,Syed A. Zaidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Monotone FedNAM 是一种物理约束的联邦加法模型，用于在不共享敏感数据的前提下实现 O-RAN 多租户场景下的可审计 SLA 违规预测。通过将具有明确物理方向的 KPI 用单调样条表示，并确保约束在联邦平均聚合中保持不变，该模型有效避免了与无线物理规律相悖的预测，提升了模型一致性与泛化能力，同时显著降低上行流量，仅轻微牺牲少量 AUC 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 O-RAN 环境中，运营商需要提前预测各切片的 SLA 违背，但不能集中收集各基站的敏感切片性能指标（KPI）。现有神经加法模型（NAM）虽具备可解释性，但其学习结果可能违背无线物理规律，如误判信道质量提升时风险上升，导致不可靠预测。因此，亟需一种既可审计又符合物理规律的分布式学习方法。

Method: 提出 Monotone FedNAM，一种基于联邦学习的加法模型。对具有明确物理意义的 KPI 使用单调样条函数建模，确保其影响趋势符合物理常识；其他不确定的 KPI 仍采用自由样条。所有约束通过设计保证在联邦平均聚合（FedAvg）过程中不被破坏，从而实现全局一致的物理合理性。模型部署于 Non-RT RIC rApp，亦可作为 Near-RT RIC xApp 轻量级运行。

Result: 在 ColO-RAN 测试集上，Monotone FedNAM 完全消除单调性违规，将受约束形状的一致性从 0.71 提升至 1.00，成功泛化到未见调度策略，上行流量减少 65%，代价仅为 AUC 损失 0.04–0.07，证明了物理约束联邦模型在多租户服务保障中的有效性与实用性。

Conclusion: 物理约束的联邦加法模型（Monotone FedNAM）能够在保护数据隐私、支持跨基站协作的同时，实现可审计且符合无线物理规律的 SLA 风险预测，是面向未来多租户 O-RAN 网络服务保障的关键技术。

Abstract: Proactive service assurance in O-RAN requires predicting per-slice SLA violations before they occur. The prediction model must be auditable by operators and must train across base stations without pooling per-slice KPIs, which are commercially sensitive because slices are leased to individual tenants. Neural additive models (NAMs) offer auditability because each KPI contributes through a visible shape function. However, visibility alone does not guarantee physical validity. On the ColO-RAN testbed dataset, unconstrained NAMs learn effects that contradict wireless physics, for example predicting higher risk when channel quality improves. This failure appears under both local and centralized training, and non-IID federated averaging worsens it. We present Monotone FedNAM, a federated additive model in which KPIs with unambiguous physical direction are represented as monotone splines whose constraints survive FedAvg aggregation by construction, while contestable KPIs remain unconstrained. The model trains and operates as a Non-RT RIC rApp and is compact enough for deployment as a Near-RT RIC xApp. Monotone FedNAM eliminates all monotonicity violations, raises constrained shape consistency from 0.71 to 1.00, generalizes to an unseen scheduling policy, and reduces uplink traffic by 65%, at a cost of 0.04 to 0.07 AUC. These results show that physically constrained federated additive models can support auditable SLA risk inference for multi-tenant O-RAN service assurance

</details>


### [96] [Neural Feature Governance: Extending Atom Prevalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21671)
*Idris Karel Seunda Ekwe,Patrick Tenga Shako,Ernest Parfait Fokoué*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经原子流行度（NAP），一种基于贝叶斯框架的结构化节点级模型选择方法，用于前馈神经网络。通过四阶段流程：贝叶斯彩票券识别、软变分训练SS-IG模型、泊松-二项分布最优层数选择与贝叶斯微调，实现稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型。在模拟非线性回归、UCI数据集（Concrete, YearPredictionMSD）和MNIST图像分类任务中验证，NAP达到最先进的结构稀疏性（如MNIST仅保留8%活跃节点），同时具备良好的不确定性校准，模型无知仅占总预测方差3-4%，预测区间覆盖率接近目标值（93.4%对95%），证明其在稀疏性、准确性、可解释性与不确定性量化方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习模型虽精度高，但存在参数量大、透明度低、计算效率差及不确定性量化不可靠等问题。亟需一种能同时实现模型压缩、可解释性提升与可靠不确定性估计的方法。

Method: NAP采用四阶段流程：1）通过迭代幅度剪枝（IMP）进行贝叶斯彩票券（BLT）识别；2）使用软变分训练方法优化Spike and Slab独立高斯（SS-IG）模型；3）利用泊松-二项分布（PB）进行最优层大小选择；4）通过贝叶斯微调生成最终稀疏、稳定、可解释且准确的模型。

Result: 在多个基准任务上，NAP显著降低模型复杂度（如MNIST中活性节点降至原始的8%），保持高精度；不确定性分析显示模型无知占比仅为3-4%，预测区间覆盖率接近名义水平（93.4%观测值对比95%目标），表现出优异的校准性能。

Conclusion: NAP是一种理论坚实、计算可行、可靠的解决方案，能够协同实现神经网络的稀疏性、准确性、可解释性与不确定性量化，推动了压缩与可解释深度学习的发展。

Abstract: Neural network compression and interpretability remain open challenges in modern deep learn- ing, where billion-parameter architectures deliver impressive accuracy at the cost of trans- parency, computational efficiency, and reliable uncertainty quantification. This paper introduces Neural Atom Prevalence (NAP), a principled Bayesian framework for structured node-level model selection in feedforward neural networks. NAP introduces the neural atom (activation unit) and functions as a hybrid method operating through a four-phase pipeline: Bayesian Lottery Ticket (BLT) identification via Iterative Magnitude Pruning (IMP), soft variational training of the Spike and Slab Independent Gaussian (SS-IG) model, Poisson-Binomial (PB) optimal layer-size selection, and Bayesian fine-tuning to produce a sparse, stable, interpretable, and accurate model. Extensive empirical validation across simulated nonlinear regression, two UCI benchmark datasets (Concrete, YearPredictionMSD), and the MNIST image classification task demonstrates that NAP achieves state-of-the-art structural sparsity, reducing active nodes to as few as 8% of the original dense architecture on MNIST, while well-calibrated probabilisti- cally: the aleatoric-epistemic uncertainty decomposition reveals that model ignorance accounts for only 3 to 4% of total predictive variance across all experiments, and regression reliability diagrams confirm a near-nominal predictive interval coverage (93.4% observed against a 95% target). These results establish NAP as a reliable, theoretically grounded, and computation- ally tractable solution to the simultaneous pursuit of sparsity, accuracy, interpretability, and uncertainty quantification in Bayesian neural networks.

</details>


### [97] [Self-Poisoning in Adaptive Out-of-Distribution Detection: A Sharp-Threshold Theory and Certified Label-Free Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21673)
*Vishnu Bindu Balachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了测试时自适应的分布外（OOD）检测器，发现其通过从无标签数据流中更新记忆库来实现。作者建立了一个广义波利亚瓮模型来描述记忆库的杂质动态，并证明了几乎必然收敛到一个均场平衡点，其斜率相当于繁殖数。当斜率低于1时，杂质保持可控；高于1时，记忆库被完全污染，检测器崩溃。实验表明，所有编码器家族的测量接纳核均为线性关系（R² ≥ 0.996），且斜率略低于1，说明这类检测器在设计上处于近临界状态。在96种设置下，预测阈值与实际崩溃点高度一致，未加门控的记忆库损失最高达0.163 AUROC。进一步提出一种认证接纳门，仅读取冻结的备用池，切断反馈回路，消除任意污染率下的相变现象，同时无需标签即可控制误报率。针对漂移导致的静态校准失败，提出CDC方法，在所有受漂移影响的场景中实现了无需标签的名义误报率恢复。最后，证明了一个双世界不可能定理：在无标签条件下，漂移与污染不可区分，从而决定了我们方法所能达到的理论性能上限。整体构建了标签自由自适应OOD检测的完整可能性/不可能性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时自适应的分布外检测器依赖无标签数据流更新记忆库，但其稳定性缺乏理论保障。尤其在高污染或数据漂移环境下，记忆库可能被污染，导致检测器失效。然而，当前方法无法判断污染与漂移的区别，也无法在无标签条件下保证可靠性。因此亟需一套理论框架来揭示此类系统的动态行为，识别其稳定边界，并设计可证明安全的改进机制。

Method: 基于广义波利亚瓮模型对记忆库杂质演化进行建模，推导出系统几乎必然收敛至均场平衡点，引入繁殖数概念分析稳定性；通过实证分析不同编码器家族的接纳核，验证系统接近临界状态；提出认证接纳门机制，利用冻结备用池切断反馈环路以防止崩溃；针对漂移问题提出CDC方法，实现无需标签的校准恢复；最后建立双世界不可能定理，揭示无标签条件下漂移与污染不可区分的本质限制。

Result: 实验表明，所有编码器家族的接纳核呈强线性关系（R² ≥ 0.996），斜率均略低于1，验证了系统近临界特性；预测阈值与实际检测器崩溃点高度吻合；未加门控的内存库在多种设置下损失高达0.163 AUROC；认证接纳门能有效防止崩溃，即使在对抗性污染下仍保持稳定；CDC方法成功恢复漂移场景下的名义误报率；理论分析确立了性能上限，为标签自由自适应检测提供了完整可能性边界。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了测试时自适应OOD检测器的内在动力学规律，证明其稳定性取决于一个类繁殖数的参数，系统在近临界状态下运行。通过引入认证接纳门和CDC方法，可在无标签条件下显著提升鲁棒性。最终的不可能定理表明，漂移与污染在无标签条件下不可区分，构成性能天花板。这些成果共同构建了标签自由自适应检测的完整理论框架，兼具实用性和理论深度。

Abstract: Test-time adaptive out-of-distribution (OOD) detectors update a memory bank from the unlabelled stream. We show this adaptation obeys a provable dynamical law. Modelling bank impurity as a generalized Pólya urn, we prove almost-sure convergence to a mean-field equilibrium whose slope acts as a reproduction number. Below one, impurity stays benign. Above one, the bank is fully poisoned and the detector collapses. The measured admission kernel is affine ($R^2 \ge 0.996$) with slope just below one in every encoder family (a protocol signature), so this detector class is near-critical by design, and across 96 settings the predicted threshold matches the empirical collapse, where ungated dictionaries lose up to $0.163$ AUROC. We then prove that a certified admission gate, reading only a frozen reserve, severs the feedback loop and removes the transition at every contamination rate, even adversarially, while controlling false positives label-free. For the complementary static-calibration failure under drift we give CDC, which restores nominal FPR label-free on all tested drift-affected cells. Finally we prove a two-world impossibility theorem. Drift and contamination are indistinguishable without labels, forcing a closed-form power ceiling our procedure approaches. Together these give a complete possibility/impossibility characterization of label-free adaptive OOD detection.

</details>


### [98] [Encoding Invisible Causation for Bridge Diagnostic Agents: Triple-Guided Retrieval-Augmented Fine-Tuning with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21680)
*Takato Yasuno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Damage Cause Encoder，通过三阶段方法实现桥梁损伤原因的自动分类：(i) 从15-35份诊断手册中提取因果三元组并存入FAISS向量库；(ii) 在训练与推理时检索相关因果三元组并拼接至损伤描述，增强模型上下文；(iii) 对比LoRA、QLoRA和QA-LoRA在固定黄金测试集上的表现，发现QLoRA在精度（87.07%）、推理速度（快11%）、显存占用（降低72%）和泛化能力上均最优。引入可复用的分层、去重、难度标注的黄金测试集作为基准，并在多样化数据上进一步验证了QLoRA的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥梁基础设施退化过程中的根本原因（如盐分侵入、冻胀、疲劳裂纹等）难以肉眼识别，专家诊断依赖多年积累的隐性知识。现有自动化系统缺乏对潜在因果关系的建模能力，亟需一种能自动推断损伤成因的方法，以支持自主桥梁诊断代理的部署。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(i) 利用大语言模型从诊断文档中提取因果三元组（损伤→导致原因），并构建FAISS向量索引；(ii) 在训练与推理过程中，通过检索机制将相关因果三元组与输入的损伤描述拼接，形成增强上下文；(iii) 系统性比较LoRA、QLoRA与QA-LoRA在固定黄金测试集上的性能，评估其精度、效率与泛化能力。

Result: QLoRA在保持与全精度LoRA相同测试准确率（87.07%）的同时，实现11%更快推理速度、72%更低GPU内存消耗，且在多样化数据集上比LoRA高出13个百分点的准确率，证明其在精度与效率之间达到最佳平衡。所提出的黄金测试集具备分层、去重、难度标签特征，可作为通用基准复用。

Conclusion: 本研究成功构建了一种高效、高精度、低资源消耗的桥梁损伤成因自动识别系统，支持在消费级硬件上边缘部署，为智能基础设施维护提供了可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Bridge infrastructure deteriorates gradually, yet its root causes---salt intrusion, freezing, fatigue cracking, and others---remain invisible to the naked eye. Expert diagnosis relies on tacit knowledge built over years of practice. We address the challenge of automating this latent causal reasoning by proposing a Damage Cause Encoder that classifies 10-class damage causes from visible damage descriptions $S_i$ for use in autonomous bridge diagnostic agents. Our approach chains three components: (i)Knowledge Triple Extraction---a large language model extracts causal triples of the form (damage $\xrightarrow{\mathtt{caused\_by}}$ cause) from 15--35 diagnostic PDF manuals and indexes them in a FAISS vector store; (ii)Retrieval-Augmented Context---at training and inference time, relevant causal triples $\mathcal{C}_i$ are retrieved and concatenated with $S_i$, converting implicit domain knowledge into explicit Encoder context; (iii)Systematic Fine-tuning Comparison---we conduct a rigorous comparison of LoRA, QLoRA, and QA-LoRA on a fixed Golden Testset (116 stratified samples), demonstrating that QLoRA achieves the optimal trade-off: identical test accuracy (87.07%) to full-precision LoRA, 11% faster inference, 72% lower GPU memory, and superior generalization across diverse unseen inputs. A controlled Golden Testset---stratified, deduplicated, and difficulty-tagged---is introduced as a reusable benchmark contribution. QLoRA further outperforms LoRA by 13 percentage points on a 100-sample diverse evaluation spanning all 10 damage cause classes.These findings enable memory-efficient, high-accuracy diagnostic agents on consumer-grade hardware for edge deployment.

</details>


### [99] [CARNet Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution for Multivariate Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21681)
*Awsaf Tausif Adib,Md. Shahria Sarker Shuvo,Md. Estehaar Ahmed Emon,Mustafa Kamal,Fuad Rahman,Shafin Rahman,Nabeel Mohammed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARNet提出一种周期条件核心聚合与重分配框架，通过多头核心聚合将全局循环信息融入高效的核心交互建模中，有效处理多变量时间序列中的跨变量依赖关系，尤其在强周期模式下表现优异。相比传统注意力机制，其保持线性复杂度，且在多个真实世界基准上显著优于Transformer和非注意力基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在建模多变量时间序列中的跨变量依赖时，虽然部分采用无注意力聚合模型以降低计算复杂度，但未能显式利用数据中存在的全局周期结构，导致预测性能受限。因此，需要一种既能保持高效计算，又能有效捕捉周期性特征的方法。

Method: 提出CARNet框架，通过周期条件核心聚合（Multihead Core Aggregation）将全局循环信息嵌入核心交互建模中，实现对周期性和跨变量依赖的联合建模，同时维持线性复杂度。

Result: 在多个真实世界多变量时间序列预测基准上，CARNet在不同预测时长下均显著优于Transformer和非注意力基线模型，证明其在准确性和可扩展性上的优势。

Conclusion: CARNet成功融合了全局周期信息与高效的线性复杂度核心交互机制，在多变量时间序列预测中展现出优越性能，为处理具有强周期性的复杂时序数据提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurately modeling cross-variate dependencies remains a key challenge in multivariate time series forecasting, particularly in the presence of strong periodic patterns. Many existing approaches rely on attention-based mechanisms that incur quadratic complexity and scale poorly with increasing numbers of variates. Recent attention-free aggregation models address this issue through linear-complexity core-based interactions, but they do not explicitly leverage the global periodic structure present in the data. To overcome this limitation, we propose CARNet, a Cycle-Conditioned Core Aggregation and Redistribution framework that integrates global recurrent cycle information into efficient core based interaction modeling via Multihead Core Aggregation. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world multivariate forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that CARNet consistently outperforms strong transformer and non-attention baselines across diverse prediction horizons while preserving linear-complexity modeling of cross-variate dependencies.

</details>


### [100] [Learning What Matters: Supervising Sparse Attention Routing with Causal Evidence Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21692)
*Jim Allchin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现，稀疏注意力机制在长上下文任务中表现不佳，因其依赖教师模型的注意力模式进行蒸馏，但注意力与实际因果依赖常不一致。通过掩码实验验证，教师模型虽能正确回答问题，却会关注过时信息；而基于因果证据训练的稀疏选择器准确率显著更高（如从41%提升至99%）。即使无需标注，仅通过掩码即可恢复相关证据，训练效果同样优秀。结果表明：注意力反映的是‘模型看哪里’，而非‘依赖什么’，因果依赖比注意力更适合作为稀疏注意力蒸馏的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前稀疏注意力机制依赖于从密集注意力模型中蒸馏注意力模式来选择关键上下文，但这一做法假设注意力模式能准确反映模型的实际依赖关系。然而，该假设在真实任务中可能不成立，尤其在检索和引用任务中，注意力与实际因果依赖存在偏差，导致蒸馏效果不佳。

Method: 通过在已知答案证据的任务中对上下文进行掩码，测试模型注意力是否与实际因果依赖一致；对比基于注意力模式与基于因果证据训练的稀疏选择器性能；利用冻结教师模型配合掩码自动提取相关证据，用于无监督训练选择器。

Result: 注意力与因果依赖存在显著冲突：教师模型虽正确作答，却常关注过时信息；基于注意力蒸馏的选择器准确率低（如41%），而基于因果证据训练的选择器可达99%，与教师性能相当；在多个预训练模型（Qwen2.5-3B、Gemma-2-9B）上均观察到类似现象。

Conclusion: 注意力不能可靠指示模型的真实依赖关系；在稀疏注意力蒸馏中，因果依赖比注意力更优的训练目标，且可通过掩码方法自动获取，无需人工标注。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long contexts by allowing each query to read only selected parts of the input. These selectors are often trained by distilling the attention patterns of a dense teacher, assuming that attention reveals which context the teacher actually uses. We test that assumption on retrieval tasks where the evidence for each answer is known exactly. By masking parts of the context and measuring whether the answer changes, we find that attention and causal dependence often disagree, and distilled selectors inherit the mismatch. Teachers attend to outdated facts they have learned to ignore, and their attention can vary across training runs even when they rely on the same evidence. In a two-step reference task, attention at the answer skips the intermediate step because it was resolved earlier in the forward pass: a selector trained on attention achieves 41% accuracy, while the same selector trained on causal evidence reaches 99% and matches the teacher. These evidence sets require no annotation: recovered from a frozen teacher by masking alone, they train selectors to the same accuracy. We find the same conflict in pretrained models: Qwen2.5-3B gives more attention to an outdated fact than the current one on 58% of conflicting-fact examples despite answering correctly, while Gemma-2-9B rises from 56% to 99% accuracy when restricted to the two relevant sentences. Attention shows where a model looks, not necessarily what its answer depends on; across the regimes we tested, that dependence matched or outperformed attention as a training target.

</details>


### [101] [An Introduction to Bayesian and Frequentist Simulation-Based Inference with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21702)
*Maximilian Dax,Theo Heimel,Gilles Louppe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于机器学习的模拟推断（SBI）在科学与工程逆问题中的应用，涵盖贝叶斯与频率学派框架，介绍了神经后验估计和神经似然估计等方法在参数估计、经验贝叶斯及去卷积任务中的使用，并讨论了结果验证与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决科学与工程中复杂的逆问题，如参数推断和探测器效应反演，传统方法难以应对高维或复杂模型，需借助机器学习提升推断效率与精度。

Method: 采用贝叶斯与频率学派统计框架，结合神经后验估计（NPE）和神经似然估计（NLE）等机器学习方法进行参数估计，同时拓展至经验贝叶斯与去卷积任务。

Result: 所提方法可有效应用于多种逆问题，具备良好的灵活性与可扩展性；但存在对数据质量依赖高、可解释性差及验证挑战等局限。

Conclusion: 基于机器学习的模拟推断是解决复杂逆问题的强大工具，但需谨慎评估其假设与验证结果，未来应加强方法的可靠性与透明度研究。

Abstract: Simulation-based inference (SBI) with machine learning is an increasingly important tool for solving inverse problems in science and engineering, including parameter inference and the inversion of detector effects. We provide an overview of the Bayesian and frequentist statistical frameworks, describe how machine-learning-based SBI methods, such as neural posterior estimation and neural likelihood estimation, can be used for parameter estimation within these frameworks, and show that the same methods can also be applied to Empirical Bayes or unfolding tasks. We also discuss how to validate inference results and the limitations of SBI with machine learning.

</details>


### [102] [A Defense of the Quadratic Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21716)
*Alexandru Meterez,Pranav Ajit Nair,Depen Morwani,Cengiz Pehlevan,Sham Kakade,Alex Damian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试了最简单的优化模型——二次模型在150M参数和30亿训练标记的LLM设置中的预测能力，发现其能准确预测长达训练周期10%的优化动态。通过泰勒展开中间检查点处的模型和损失函数，结合拉克斯科茨四重积分法深入估计海森矩阵谱，揭示了特征值和特征向量中存在显著结构，且受批大小、预处理方式和训练时间影响。此外，通过实证检验中间检查点的局部线性稳定性，发现大模型优化通常处于随机稳定边缘，其性质也由批大小决定。结果表明二次模型可作为预训练优化动态的理论可处理代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化理论依赖理想化模型，但这些模型在理论上易处理与真实优化动态描述之间存在权衡。为突破这一困境，本文尝试检验最简模型——二次模型在实际大语言模型训练中的有效性。

Method: 采用泰勒展开方法对训练过程中的中间检查点进行模型与损失函数的局部近似；利用拉克斯科茨四重积分法深度探测海森矩阵谱；通过实证分析局部线性稳定性，并与理论预测对比。

Result: 二次模型在150M参数、30亿训练样本的LLM场景下具有强预测能力，可准确描述长达10%训练周期的优化动态；海森矩阵谱呈现显著结构，受批大小、预处理和训练时间影响；优化过程通常位于随机稳定边缘，其特性由批大小决定。

Conclusion: 二次模型虽简单，但在大语言模型预训练优化中表现出良好的理论可处理性和预测能力，可作为研究优化动态的有力工具。

Abstract: Due to the complexity of neural network loss landscapes, optimization theory is forced to rely on idealized models, and there is generally a tradeoff between how theoretically tractable the model is, and how accurately it describes the true optimization dynamics. In this work, we stress test the simplest possible model of optimization -- the quadratic model -- and show that it can be surprisingly predictive in an LLM setting with 150M parameters and 3B training tokens. Specifically, we show that Taylor expanding the model and the loss function at intermediate checkpoints through training can accurately predict the optimization dynamics over windows that can last up to 10\% of training. Having established this agreement, we then turn to analyzing the structure of these local quadratic optimization problems through two lenses: the Hessian spectrum and local stability.
